   00022: function isYmd(value) {
   00023:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(
   00024:     String(value || "").trim()
   00025:   );
   00026: }
   00027: 
   00028: function ymdToUtc(value) {
   00029:   if (!isYmd(value)) return null;
   00030: 
   00031:   const [year, month, day] = value
   00032:     .split("-")
   00033:     .map(Number);
   00034: 
   00035:   return new Date(
   00036:     Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day)
   00037:   );
   00038: }
   00039: 
   00040: function addDays(value, amount) {
   00041:   const date = ymdToUtc(value);
   00042: 
   00043:   if (!date) return value;
   00044: 
   00045:   date.setUTCDate(
   00046:     date.getUTCDate() + Number(amount || 0)
   00047:   );
   00048: 
   00049:   return [
   00050:     date.getUTCFullYear(),
   00051:     pad2(date.getUTCMonth() + 1),
   00052:     pad2(date.getUTCDate()),
   00053:   ].join("-");
   00054: }
   00055: 
>> 00056: function splitRange(dateFrom, dateTo) {
   00057:   const chunks = [];
   00058: 
>> 00059:   if (!isYmd(dateFrom) || !isYmd(dateTo)) {
   00060:     return chunks;
   00061:   }
   00062: 
>> 00063:   let cursor = dateFrom;
   00064: 
>> 00065:   while (cursor <= dateTo) {
>> 00066:     const candidateTo = addDays(
   00067:       cursor,
   00068:       CHUNK_DAYS - 1
   00069:     );
   00070: 
   00071:     const chunkTo =
>> 00072:       candidateTo > dateTo
>> 00073:         ? dateTo
>> 00074:         : candidateTo;
   00075: 
   00076:     chunks.push({
   00077:       from: cursor,
   00078:       to: chunkTo,
   00079:     });
   00080: 
   00081:     cursor = addDays(chunkTo, 1);
   00082:   }
   00083: 
   00084:   return chunks;
   00085: }
   00086: 
   00087: function firstValidYmd(...values) {

   00094:       return normalized;
   00095:     }
   00096:   }
   00097: 
   00098:   return "";
   00099: }

   00159: function collectPrizes(raw) {
   00160:   const flat = normalizeFlatPrizes(raw);
   00161: 
   00162:   if (flat.length) {
   00163:     return flat;
   00164:   }
   00165:

   00171:       : [];
   00172: 
   00173:     for (const prize of prizes) {
   00174:       output.push(prize);
   00175:     }
   00176:   }
   00177: 
   00178:   return output;
   00179: }

   00383: function formatScore(value) {
   00384:   const number = Number(value);
   00385: 
   00386:   if (!Number.isFinite(number)) {
   00387:     return "0,000";
   00388:   }
   00389: 
   00390:   return number.toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
   00391:     minimumFractionDigits: 3,
   00392:     maximumFractionDigits: 3,
   00393:   });
   00394: }
   00395: 
   00396: function formatPercent(value) {
   00397:   const number = Number(value);
   00398: 
   00399:   if (!Number.isFinite(number)) {
   00400:     return "0,00%";
   00401:   }
   00402: 
   00403:   return (number * 100).toLocaleString(
   00404:     "pt-BR",
   00405:     {
   00406:       minimumFractionDigits: 2,
   00407:       maximumFractionDigits: 2,
   00408:     }
   00409:   ) + "%";
   00410: }

   00001: // src/pages/Centenas/centenas.utils.js
   00002: // Helpers puros (sem React) extraídos de Centenas.jsx
   00003: 
   00004: export function pad2(n) {
   00005:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00006: }
   00007: 
   00008: /**
   00009:  * ✅ YYYY-MM-DD estrito (formato + data válida)
   00010:  * - evita aceitar 2026-99-99, 2026-02-31 etc
   00011:  */
   00012: export function isYMD(s) {
   00013:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
   00014:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
   00015: 
   00016:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
   00017:   if (!Number.isFinite(y) || !Number.isFinite(m) || !Number.isFinite(d)) return false;
   00018:   if (m < 1 || m > 12) return false;
   00019:   if (d < 1 || d > 31) return false;
   00020: 
   00021:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   00022:   return (
   00023:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
   00024:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
   00025:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
   00026:   );
   00027: }
   00028: 
>> 00029: export function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   00030:   const m = String(ymd || "").trim().match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00031:   if (!m) return "";
   00032:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
   00033: }
   00034: 
   00035: export function ymdToUTCDate(ymd) {
   00036:   const m = String(ymd || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00037:   if (!m) return null;
   00038:   return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
   00039: }
   00040: 
   00041: export function addDaysUTC(ymd, days) {
   00042:   const dt = ymdToUTCDate(ymd);
   00043:   if (!dt) return ymd;
   00044:   dt.setUTCDate(dt.getUTCDate() + Number(days || 0));
   00045:   const y = dt.getUTCFullYear();
   00046:   const m = pad2(dt.getUTCMonth() + 1);
   00047:   const d = pad2(dt.getUTCDate());
   00048:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
   00049: }
   00050: 
   00051: export function todayYMDLocal() {
   00052:   const d = new Date();
   00053:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00054: }
   00055: 
   00056: export function sortPTBR(a, b) {
   00057:   return String(a).localeCompare(String(b), "pt-BR", { sensitivity: "base" });
   00058: }
   00059: 
   00060: export function isTodos(v) {
   00061:   return String(v || "").trim().toLowerCase() === "todos";
   00062: }
   00063: 
   00064: /**
   00065:  * Normaliza "hora" em formato HH:MM (quando possível)
   00066:  */
   00067: export function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00068:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00069:   if (!s0) return "";
   00070:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00071: 
   00072:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00073:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   00074: 
   00075:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00076:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   00077: 
   00078:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00079:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   00080: 
   00081:   return s0.trim();
   00082: }
   00083: 
   00084: /**
   00085:  * Bucketiza para HH:00, ignorando minutos/segundos.
   00086:  * Ex: "21:10" -> "21:00", "21h" -> "21:00", "09:00" -> "09:00"
   00087:  */
   00088: export function toHourBucketHH00(value) {
   00089:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(value);
   00090:   if (!norm) return "";
   00091:   const m = String(norm).match(/^(\d{2})/);
   00092:   if (!m) return "";
   00093:   const hh = m[1];
   00094:   const n = Number(hh);
   00095:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 23) return "";
   00096:   return `${hh}:00`;
   00097: }
   00098: 
   00099: export function extractHourFromText(text) {
   00100:   const s = String(text ?? "").trim();
   00101:   if (!s) return "";
   00102:   const mh = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2})\s*(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)\b/i);
   00103:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}:00`;
   00104:   const mIso = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?\b/);
   00105:   if (mIso) return `${pad2(mIso[1])}:${pad2(mIso[2])}`;
   00106:   const mBare = s.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})(?:\D|$)/);
   00107:   if (mBare) {
   00108:     const n = Number(mBare[1]);

   00046:   const parsed = Number(value);
   00047:

   00049:     ? parsed
   00050:     : fallback;
   00051: }

   00053: function firstValue(object, keys) {
   00054:   for (const key of keys) {
   00055:     const value = object?.[key];
   00056: 
   00057:     if (
   00058:       value !== undefined &&
   00059:       value !== null &&
   00060:       String(value).trim() !== ""
   00061:     ) {
   00062:       return value;
   00063:     }
   00064:   }
   00065: 
   00066:   return "";
   00067: }
   00068: 
   00069: export function normalizeCentena3V3(value) {
   00070:   const digits = digitsOnly(value);
   00071: 
   00072:   if (!digits) return "";
   00073: 
   00074:   return digits.slice(-3).padStart(3, "0");
   00075: }

   00081: 
   00082:   return digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
   00083: }

>> 00475: function clampRangeToBounds(next, minDate, maxDate) {
   00476:   const f = next?.from;
   00477:   const t = next?.to;
   00478: 
   00479:   if (!isISODate(f) || !isISODate(t)) return null;
>> 00480:   if (!minDate || !maxDate) return null;
>> 00481:   if (!isISODate(minDate) || !isISODate(maxDate)) return null;
   00482: 
>> 00483:   let from = f < minDate ? minDate : f;
>> 00484:   let to = t > maxDate ? maxDate : t;
   00485: 
   00486:   if (from > to) to = from;
   00487:   return { from, to };
   00488: }

   00490: function normalizeLoteriaKey(v) {
   00491:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00492: 
   00493:   if (!raw) return "PT_RIO";
   00494: 
   00495:   const key = raw
   00496:     .toLowerCase()
   00497:     .normalize("NFD")
   00498:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
   00499:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   00500:     .trim();
   00501: 
   00502:   if (key === "federal" || key === "fed" || key === "br" || key === "brasil") return "FEDERAL";
   00503:   if (key === "rj" || key === "rio" || key === "pt_rio" || key === "pt-rio") return "PT_RIO";
   00504: 
   00505:   const out = key
   00506:     .toUpperCase()
   00507:     .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "_")
   00508:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
   00509: 
   00510:   return out || "PT_RIO";
   00511: }

   00521:     return "2022-02-08";
   00522:   }
   00523:

   00525: }

   00529:   if (ymd && isISODate(ymd)) return ymd;
   00530: 
   00531:   const now = new Date();
   00532:   const yyyy = now.getFullYear();
   00533:   const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
   00534:   const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
   00535:   return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`;
   00536: }
   00537: 
   00538: function yearRangeToDates(minY, maxY, floorISO, ceilISO) {
   00539:   const y1 = Number(minY);
   00540:   const y2 = Number(maxY);
   00541:   if (!Number.isFinite(y1) || !Number.isFinite(y2)) return null;
   00542: 
   00543:   const a = Math.min(y1, y2);
   00544:   const b = Math.max(y1, y2);
   00545: 
   00546:   let from = `${a}-01-01`;
   00547:   let to = `${b}-12-31`;
   00548: 
   00549:   if (isISODate(floorISO) && from < floorISO) from = floorISO;
   00550:   if (isISODate(ceilISO) && to > ceilISO) to = ceilISO;
   00551: 
   00552:   if (isISODate(from) && isISODate(to) && from > to) to = from;
   00553:   return { from, to };
   00554: }
   00555:

   00562:     const hh = String(bucket).replace(/\D/g, "").padStart(2, "0");
   00563:     return `${hh}:00`;

   00030: function clamp(n, a, b) {
   00031:   return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
   00032: }
   00033: 
   00034: function pad2(n) {
   00035:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00036: }
   00037: 
   00038: /** ✅ Normaliza "qualquer data" para YYYY-MM-DD (ou null). */
   00039: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
   00040:   if (!input) return null;
   00041: 
   00042:   // Firestore Timestamp com toDate()
   00043:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
   00044:     const d = input.toDate();
   00045:     if (d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00046:       return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
   00047:         d.getUTCDate()
   00048:       )}`;
   00049:     }
   00050:   }
   00051: 
   00052:   // Timestamp-like { seconds } / { _seconds }
   00053:   if (
   00054:     typeof input === "object" &&
   00055:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) ||
   00056:       Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
   00057:   ) {
   00058:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds))
   00059:       ? Number(input.seconds)
   00060:       : Number(input._seconds);
   00061: 
   00062:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
   00063:     if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00064:       return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
   00065:         d.getUTCDate()
   00066:       )}`;
   00067:     }
   00068:   }
   00069: 
   00070:   // Date
   00071:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
   00072:     return `${input.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(input.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
   00073:       input.getUTCDate()
   00074:     )}`;
   00075:   }
   00076: 
   00077:   const s = String(input).trim();
   00078:   if (!s) return null;
   00079: 
   00080:   // YYYY-MM-DD...
   00081:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
   00082:   if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;
   00083: 
   00084:   // DD/MM/YYYY
   00085:   const br = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
   00086:   if (br) return `${br[3]}-${br[2]}-${br[1]}`;
   00087: 
   00088:   return null;
   00089: }

--- LINHAS 1 A 93 ---

   00001: // src/pages/Dashboard/components/DateRangeControl.jsx
   00002: import React, { useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
   00003: 
   00004: /**
   00005:  * DateRangeControl (Premium, sem libs) — CONTROLADO DE VERDADE
   00006:  *
   00007:  * Regra:
   00008:  * - NÃO chama onChange() sozinho via useEffect.
   00009:  * - Só chama onChange() quando o usuário interage (inputs/slider/chips).
>> 00010:  * - minDate/maxDate devem vir do pai para o SLIDER funcionar bem.
   00011:  *
   00012:  * Importante:
>> 00013:  * - Inputs de data NÃO dependem de boundsReady (para o calendário nunca “morrer”).
>> 00014:  * - Se bounds não existirem, o componente ainda permite selecionar datas e chama onChange.
   00015:  *
   00016:  * Ajuste atual:
   00017:  * - Chips de anos: ✅ 1 linha e ocupa 100% (sem espaço vazio à direita)
   00018:  * - Compactação vertical (para caber no topo 250px com KPI sem cortar)
   00019:  *   mantendo o mesmo visual premium.
   00020:  *
   00021:  * Fix UX (parece "Todos" + outro ano selecionado):
   00022:  * - Remove “efeito de seleção” vindo de foco (focus/focus-visible) no chip.
   00023:  * - Após clicar, o chip dá blur para não ficar marcado.
   00024:  * - Seleção visual fica SOMENTE pelo chipActive.
   00025:  *
   00026:  * ✅ DEMO safe:
   00027:  * - prop `disabled`: trava inputs / slider / chips / calendário
   00028:  * - prop `onBlocked(kind)`: callback quando tenta interagir bloqueado
   00029:  */
   00030: 
   00031: function pad2(n) {
   00032:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00033: }
   00034: 
   00035: function isISODate(s) {
   00036:   return /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.test(String(s || ""));
   00037: }
   00038: 
   00039: function formatBR(iso) {
   00040:   if (!isISODate(iso)) return "";
   00041:   const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-");
   00042:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
   00043: }
   00044: 
   00045: function toUTCDate(iso) {
   00046:   if (!isISODate(iso)) return null;
   00047:   const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
   00048:   if (!y || !m || !d) return null;
   00049:   return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   00050: }
   00051: 
   00052: function fromUTCDate(dt) {
   00053:   if (!(dt instanceof Date) || !Number.isFinite(dt.getTime())) return null;
   00054:   const y = dt.getUTCFullYear();
   00055:   const m = pad2(dt.getUTCMonth() + 1);
   00056:   const d = pad2(dt.getUTCDate());
   00057:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
   00058: }
   00059: 
   00060: function clamp(n, a, b) {
   00061:   return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
   00062: }
   00063: 
   00064: function clampDateISO(iso, minISO, maxISO) {
   00065:   const dt = toUTCDate(iso);
   00066:   const minDt = toUTCDate(minISO);
   00067:   const maxDt = toUTCDate(maxISO);
   00068: 
   00069:   if (!minDt || !maxDt) return isISODate(iso) ? iso : null;
   00070:   if (!dt) return maxISO;
   00071: 
   00072:   const clamped = new Date(clamp(dt.getTime(), minDt.getTime(), maxDt.getTime()));
   00073:   return fromUTCDate(clamped) || maxISO;
   00074: }
   00075: 
   00076: function daysBetweenUTC(aISO, bISO) {
   00077:   const a = toUTCDate(aISO);
   00078:   const b = toUTCDate(bISO);
   00079:   if (!a || !b) return 0;
   00080:   const ms = b.getTime() - a.getTime();
   00081:   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return 0;
   00082:   return Math.floor(ms / 86400000);
   00083: }
   00084: 
   00085: function addDaysUTC(iso, days) {
   00086:   const dt = toUTCDate(iso);
   00087:   if (!dt) return iso;
   00088:   dt.setUTCDate(dt.getUTCDate() + Number(days || 0));
   00089:   return fromUTCDate(dt);
   00090: }

   00153:   const rawTo = isISODate(value?.to) ? value.to : "";
   00154: 
   00155:   const fireBlocked = useCallback(
   00156:     (kind, e) => {
   00157:       if (!disabled) return false;
   00158:       try {
   00159:         e?.preventDefault?.();
   00160:         e?.stopPropagation?.();

   00162:       if (typeof onBlocked === "function") onBlocked(kind);
   00163:       return true;
   00164:     },
   00165:     [disabled, onBlocked]
   00166:   );
   00167: 
>> 00168:   // fallback visual quando bounds existem (sem auto-commit)
   00169:   const viewFrom = useMemo(() => {
>> 00170:     if (!boundsReady) return rawFrom || "";
   00171:     if (!rawFrom) return maxISO;
   00172:     return clampDateISO(rawFrom, minISO, maxISO);
>> 00173:   }, [boundsReady, rawFrom, minISO, maxISO]);
   00174: 
   00175:   const viewTo = useMemo(() => {
>> 00176:     if (!boundsReady) return rawTo || "";

>> 00183:     if (!boundsReady) return { from: rawFrom || "", to: rawTo || "" };

   00187:     if (!f || !t) return { from: maxISO, to: maxISO };
   00188: 
   00189:     if (f.getTime() <= t.getTime()) return { from: viewFrom, to: viewTo };
   00190:     return { from: viewTo, to: viewFrom };
>> 00191:   }, [boundsReady, rawFrom, rawTo, viewFrom, viewTo, maxISO]);
   00192: 
   00193:   const totalDays = useMemo(() => {
>> 00194:     if (!boundsReady) return 1;
   00195:     return Math.max(1, daysBetweenUTC(minISO, maxISO));
>> 00196:   }, [boundsReady, minISO, maxISO]);
   00197: 
   00198:   const fromOffset = useMemo(() => {
>> 00199:     if (!boundsReady) return 0;

>> 00201:   }, [boundsReady, minISO, normalized.from, totalDays]);
   00202: 
   00203:   const toOffset = useMemo(() => {
>> 00204:     if (!boundsReady) return 0;

>> 00206:   }, [boundsReady, minISO, normalized.to, totalDays]);
   00207: 
   00208:   const label = useMemo(() => {

   00211:   }, [normalized.from, normalized.to]);
   00212:

   00069: /** Normaliza posição para "1º..7º" ou "Todos" */
   00070: function normalizePosicaoInput(v) {
   00071:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00072:   if (!s) return "Todos";
   00073:   if (s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return "Todos";
   00074: 
   00075:   const mNum = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00076:   if (mNum) return `${Number(mNum[1])}º`;
   00077: 
   00078:   const mOrd = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*º$/);
   00079:   if (mOrd) return `${Number(mOrd[1])}º`;
   00080: 
   00081:   return s;
   00082: }

   00133: /** Garante que o value exista na lista */
   00134: function ensureValueInList({
   00135:   value,
   00136:   list,
   00137:   forceValue = null,
   00138:   fallback = "Todos",
   00139:   normalizeFn = null,
   00140: }) {
   00141:   const base = forceValue ?? value;
   00142:   const v0 = normalizeValue(base, fallback);
   00143:   const v = typeof normalizeFn === "function" ? normalizeFn(v0) : v0;
   00144: 
   00145:   const arr = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
   00146:   if (!arr.length) return v;
   00147: 
   00148:   const isObjList = typeof arr[0] === "object" && arr[0] && "value" in arr[0];
   00149: 
   00150:   const norm = (x) => {
   00151:     const s = normalizeValue(x, "");
   00152:     return typeof normalizeFn === "function" ? normalizeFn(s) : s;
   00153:   };
   00154: 
   00155:   if (isObjList) {
   00156:     const values = new Set(arr.map((x) => norm(x?.value)));
   00157:     if (values.has(v)) return v;
   00158:     if (values.has("Todos")) return "Todos";
   00159:     const first = norm(arr[0]?.value);
   00160:     return first || fallback;
   00161:   }
   00162: 
   00163:   const values = new Set(arr.map((x) => norm(x)));
   00164:   if (values.has(v)) return v;
   00165:   if (values.has("Todos")) return "Todos";
   00166: 
   00167:   const first = norm(arr[0]);
   00168:   return first || fallback;
   00169: }

   00568: 
   00569:   const handleChange = useCallback(
   00570:     (name, next) => {
   00571:       if (disabledAll) {
   00572:         if (typeof onBlocked === "function") onBlocked(name);
   00573:         return;
   00574:       }
   00575: 
   00576:       // ✅ Troca loteria: normaliza e reseta filtros sensíveis SEMPRE
   00577:       if (name === "loteria") {
   00578:         const nextLot = normalizeLoteriaInput(next);
   00579:         onChange("loteria", nextLot);
   00580: 
   00581:         // reset para evitar “estatística inválida” por estado anterior
   00582:         onChange("horario", "Todos");
   00583:         onChange("animal", "Todos");
   00584:         onChange("posicao", "Todos");
   00585: 
   00586:         return;
   00587:       }
   00588: 
   00589:       if (lockPosicaoV1 && name === "posicao") {
   00590:         onChange("posicao", "1º");
   00591:         return;
   00592:       }
   00593: 
   00594:       if (name === "posicao") {
   00595:         onChange(name, normalizePosicaoInput(next));
   00596:         return;
   00597:       }
   00598: 
>> 00599:       if (name === "diaSemana") {
>> 00600:         onChange(name, normalizeDiaSemanaInput(next));
   00601:         return;
   00602:       }
   00603: 
   00604:       onChange(name, next);
   00605:     },
   00606:     [disabledAll, onBlocked, lockPosicaoV1, onChange]
   00607:   );
   00608: 
   00609:   const Item = ({
   00610:     label,
   00611:     value,
   00612:     name,
   00613:     list,
   00614:     wrapClassName,

   00620:       name === "posicao"
   00621:         ? normalizePosicaoInput
>> 00622:         : name === "diaSemana"
>> 00623:         ? normalizeDiaSemanaInput
   00624:         : name === "loteria"
   00625:         ? normalizeLoteriaInput
   00626:         : null;
   00627: 
   00628:     const normalizedValue = ensureValueInList({
   00629:       value,
   00630:       list,
   00631:       forceValue,
   00632:       fallback: name === "loteria" ? "PT_RIO" : "Todos",
   00633:       normalizeFn,
   00634:     });
   00635: 
   00636:     const isObjList =
   00637:       Array.isArray(list) && list.length && typeof list[0] === "object" && list[0];
   00638: 
   00639:     const finalDisabled = !!disabledAll || !!disabled;
   00640: 
   00641:     const block = (e) => {
   00642:       if (!finalDisabled) return;
   00643:       try {
   00644:         e?.preventDefault?.();
   00645:         e?.stopPropagation?.();
   00646:       } catch {}
   00647:       if (typeof onBlocked === "function") onBlocked(name);
   00648:     };

   00650:     return (

   00654:             {label}

   00656:

   00659:               value={normalizedValue}

   00666:                 if (!finalDisabled) return;
   00667:                 block(e);
   00668:               }}

   00670:               style={{
   00671:                 ...ui.select,
   00672:                 ...(finalDisabled ? ui.selectDisabled : null),
   00673:               }}
   00674:             >

   00676:                 ? list.map((opt, idx) => (

   00678:                       {opt?.label}
   00679:                     </option>
   00680:                   ))
   00681:                 : (list || ["Todos"]).map((opt, idx) => (
   00682:                     <option key={`${name}_${String(opt)}_${idx}`} value={opt}>
   00683:                       {opt}
   00684:                     </option>
   00685:                   ))}
   00686:             </select>

   00689:           </div>
   00690:         </div>
   00691:       </div>
   00692:     );
   00693:   };
   00694:

   00696:   const horarioForcedValue = null;
   00697:   const horarioDisabled = false;
   00698: 
   00699:   return (

   00702:

   00004: /**
   00005:  * KPI cards (top right) — premium executivo (legível e responsivo)
   00006:  * - Mantém compatibilidade: items / columns / gap / drawsRaw
   00007:  * - Se items vier faltando/ruim, calcula via drawsRaw:
>> 00008:  *   - Sorteios = drawsRaw.length
>> 00009:  *   - Dias = unique dates (robusto)
   00010:  *
   00011:  * ✅ Ajuste desta versão:
   00012:  * - Remove bolinha dourada ao lado do número
   00013:  * - Centraliza TÍTULO + NÚMERO (horizontalmente) dentro do card
>> 00014:  * - Mantém o “desce levemente” do número para casar com o fim do banner
   00015:  * - Mantém tipografia responsiva com clamp()
   00016:  * - Mantém layout estável sem “explodir” altura
   00017:  *
   00018:  * ✅ NOVO (padrão ChartsGrid):
   00019:  * - drawsRaw = VIEW (filtrado)
   00020:  * - drawsRawGlobal = GLOBAL (sem filtros) [opcional]
   00021:  * - showGlobalAparicoes (default false): adiciona KPI "Aparições (Geral)" baseado em prizes do GLOBAL.
   00022:  */
   00023: 
   00024: function pad2(n) {
   00025:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00026: }
   00027: 
   00028: /**
   00029:  * ✅ FIX (UTC-safe):
   00030:  * Gera YMD usando getters UTC para evitar "virar o dia" por timezone (-03).
   00031:  */
   00032: function ymdFromDateUTC(d) {
   00033:   return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getUTCDate())}`;
   00034: }
   00035: 
   00036: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
   00037:   if (!input) return null;
   00038: 
   00039:   // Firestore Timestamp-like: { toDate() }
   00040:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
   00041:     const d = input.toDate();
   00042:     if (d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00043:       return ymdFromDateUTC(d);
   00044:     }
   00045:   }
   00046: 
   00047:   // Timestamp-like: { seconds } / { _seconds }
   00048:   if (
   00049:     typeof input === "object" &&
   00050:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) || Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
   00051:   ) {
   00052:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds))
   00053:       ? Number(input.seconds)
   00054:       : Number(input._seconds);
   00055: 
   00056:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
   00057:     if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00058:       return ymdFromDateUTC(d);
   00059:     }
   00060:   }
   00061: 
   00062:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
   00063:     return ymdFromDateUTC(input);
   00064:   }
   00065: 
   00066:   const s = String(input).trim();
   00067:   if (!s) return null;
   00068: 
   00069:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
   00070:   if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;
   00071: 
   00072:   const br = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
   00073:   if (br) return `${br[3]}-${br[2]}-${br[1]}`;
   00074: 
   00075:   return null;
   00076: }
   00077: 
   00078: function getDrawDate(draw) {
   00079:   if (!draw) return null;
   00080: 
   00081:   const raw =
   00082:     draw.date ??
   00083:     draw.ymd ??
   00084:     draw.draw_date ??
   00085:     draw.drawDate ??
   00086:     draw.close_date ??
   00087:     draw.data ??
   00088:     draw.dt ??
   00089:     draw.day ??
   00090:     null;
   00091: 
   00092:   return normalizeToYMD(raw);
   00093: }

   00136:    GLOBAL (Aparições) — prizes

   00139: function countAparicoesGlobal(drawsRawGlobal) {
   00140:   const draws = Array.isArray(drawsRawGlobal) ? drawsRawGlobal : [];
   00141:   let apar = 0;
   00142: 
   00143:   for (const d of draws) {
   00144:     const prizes = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
   00145:     apar += prizes.length;
   00146:   }
   00147: 
   00148:   return apar;
   00149: }

   00028: /* =========================
   00029:    Hora: padronizar para bucket "09h", "11h"...

   00031: function hourBucketFromAny(value) {
   00032:   const s0 = safeStr(value);
   00033:   if (!s0) return "";
   00034: 
   00035:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00036: 
   00037:   // "11h", "11hs", "11hr"...
   00038:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00039:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}h`;
   00040: 
   00041:   // "11:10" ou "11:00" ou "11:10:00"
   00042:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?$/);
   00043:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}h`;
   00044: 
   00045:   // "11"
   00046:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00047:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}h`;
   00048: 
   00049:   return s0; // fallback (não deveria)
   00050: }
   00051: 
   00052: /**
   00053:  * ✅ Base URL para assets do public/
   00054:  * - CRA: process.env.PUBLIC_URL
   00055:  * - fallback: "" (raiz)
   00056:  */
   00057: function publicBase() {
   00058:   const b = String(process.env.PUBLIC_URL || "").trim();
   00059:   return b && b !== "/" ? b : "";
   00060: }
   00061: 
   00062: /**
   00063:  * ✅ Normaliza caminhos de imagem para o React (public/)
   00064:  */
   00065: function normalizeImgSrc(src) {
   00066:   const s = safeStr(src);
   00067:   if (!s) return "";
   00068: 
   00069:   if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(s)) return s;
   00070: 
   00071:   const base = publicBase();
   00072: 
   00073:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return `${base}${s}`;
   00074: 
   00075:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return `${base}/${s.slice("public/".length)}`;
   00076:   if (s.startsWith("img/")) return `${base}/${s}`;
   00077: 
   00078:   return `${base}/${s}`;
   00079: }

   00045: 
   00046:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00047:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   00048: 
   00049:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00050:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   00051: 
   00052:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00053:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   00054: 
   00055:   return s0;
   00056: }
   00057: 
   00058: function toHourBucket(hhmm) {
   00059:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
   00060:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00061:   if (!m) return s;
   00062:   return `${m[1]}:00`;
   00063: }
   00064: 
   00065: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
   00066:   if (!input) return null;
   00067: 
   00068:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
   00069:     const d = input.toDate();
   00070:     if (d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00071:       return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00072:     }
   00073:   }
   00074: 
   00075:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
   00076:     return `${input.getFullYear()}-${pad2(input.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(input.getDate())}`;
   00077:   }
   00078: 
   00079:   const s = safeStr(input);
   00080:   if (!s) return null;
   00081: 
   00082:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
   00083:   if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;
   00084: 
   00085:   const br = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
   00086:   if (br) return `${br[3]}-${br[2]}-${br[1]}`;
   00087: 
   00088:   return null;
   00089: }
   00090: 
   00091: function todayYMDLocal() {
   00092:   const d = new Date();
   00093:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00094: }
   00095: 
   00096: function getDowKey(ymd) {
   00097:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
   00098:   const [Y, M, D] = ymd.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
   00099:   const dt = new Date(Y, M - 1, D);
   00100:   return dt.getDay();
   00101: }
   00102: 
   00103: function getMonthNum(ymd) {
   00104:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
   00105:   const m = ymd.match(/^\d{4}-(\d{2})-\d{2}$/);
   00106:   if (!m) return null;
   00107:   return Number(m[1]);
   00108: }
   00109: 
   00110: function getDayNum(ymd) {
   00111:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
   00112:   const m = ymd.match(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-(\d{2})$/);
   00113:   if (!m) return null;

   00174:   return normalizeHourLike(draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || "");
   00175: }

   00181:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.data) ||
   00182:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.dt) ||

   00185: }

   00189: function normalizeUfToQueryKey(input) {
   00190:   const s = safeStr(input).toUpperCase();
   00191:   if (!s) return "";
   00192:   if (s.includes("_") || s.length > 2) return s;
   00193:   return UF_TO_LOTTERY_KEY[s] || s;
   00194: }
   00195: 
   00196: function lotteryLabelFromKey(key) {
   00197:   const s = safeStr(key).toUpperCase();
   00198:   if (s === "PT_RIO") return "RIO";
   00199:   if (s === "FEDERAL") return "FEDERAL";
   00200:   if (s.length === 2) return s;
   00201:   const parts = s.split("_");
   00202:   return parts[parts.length - 1] || s;
   00203: }

>> 00209: function clampYmd(ymd, minDate, maxDate) {
   00210:   const d = normalizeToYMD(ymd);
   00211:   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
   00212: 
   00213:   let out = d;
   00214: 
>> 00215:   if (isYMD(minDate) && out < minDate) out = minDate;
>> 00216:   if (isYMD(maxDate) && out > maxDate) out = maxDate;
   00217: 
   00218:   return out;
   00219: }

>> 00221: function normalizeRangeWithBounds(fromIn, toIn, minDate, maxDate) {
>> 00222:   let from = clampYmd(fromIn, minDate, maxDate);
>> 00223:   let to = clampYmd(toIn, minDate, maxDate);
   00224: 
>> 00225:   const fallback = clampYmd(todayYMDLocal(), minDate, maxDate) || maxDate || minDate || todayYMDLocal();
   00226: 
   00227:   if (!from) from = fallback;
   00228:   if (!to) to = from;
   00229: 
   00230:   if (from > to) [from, to] = [to, from];
   00231: 
   00232:   return { from, to };
   00233: }

   00239: function downloadBlob(filename, blob) {
   00240:   const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
   00241:   const a = document.createElement("a");
   00242:   a.href = url;
   00243:   a.download = filename;
   00244:   document.body.appendChild(a);
   00245:   a.click();
   00246:   a.remove();
   00247:   URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   00248: }

   00250: async function exportExcelXlsx({
   00251:   filename,
   00252:   title,
   00253:   metaLines,
   00254:   columns,
   00255:   rows,
   00256:   textCols,
   00257:   sheetName = "resultados",
   00258: }) {
   00259:   const mod = await import("xlsx");
   00260:   const XLSX = mod?.default ?? mod;
   00261: 
   00262:   const aoa = [];
   00263:   aoa.push([String(title || "Exportação")]);
   00264:   (metaLines || []).forEach((l) => aoa.push([String(l || "")]));
   00265:   aoa.push([]);
   00266:   aoa.push(columns.map((c) => String(c ?? "")));
   00267:   rows.forEach((r) => aoa.push((r || []).map((v) => String(v ?? ""))));
   00268: 
   00269:   const ws = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(aoa);
   00270: 
   00271:   // linhas acima dos dados:
   00272:   // title(1) + meta(n) + blank(1) + header(1) => firstDataRowAoa = n + 3 (0-based)
   00273:   const headerRowNumber = 1 + (metaLines?.length || 0) + 1 + 1;
   00274:   ws["!freeze"] = { xSplit: 0, ySplit: headerRowNumber };
   00275: 
   00276:   const firstDataRowAoa = (metaLines?.length || 0) + 3;
   00277:   const tcols = Array.isArray(textCols) ? textCols : [];
   00278: 
   00279:   if (tcols.length) {
   00280:     for (let r = 0; r < rows.length; r += 1) {
   00281:       const aoaRowIndex = firstDataRowAoa + r;
   00282:       for (const c of tcols) {
   00283:         const cellAddr = XLSX.utils.encode_cell({ r: aoaRowIndex, c });
   00284:         const cell = ws[cellAddr];
   00285:         if (!cell) continue;
   00286:         cell.t = "s";
   00287:         cell.z = "@";
   00288:         cell.v = String(cell.v ?? "");
   00289:       }
   00290:     }
   00291:   }
   00292: 
   00293:   const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
   00294:   XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, sheetName);
   00295: 
   00296:   const array = XLSX.write(wb, { bookType: "xlsx", type: "array" });
   00297: 
   00298:   const blob = new Blob([array], {
   00299:     type: "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
   00300:   });
   00301: 
   00302:   downloadBlob(filename, blob);
   00303: }

>> 00021:  * - Rebusca bounds periodicamente (maxYmd atualiza quando entra sorteio novo)
>> 00022:  * - Detecta "último sorteio importado" (maxYmd + maior close_hour do dia)
>> 00023:  * - Se mudar maxYmd ou close_hour do último sorteio -> refresh automático
   00024:  *
   00025:  * ✅ IMPORTANTE (SEU PROJETO):
   00026:  * - Consultas devem usar UF="RJ" (com trava interna para PT_RIO)
   00027:  */
   00028: 
   00029: function pad2(n) {
   00030:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00031: }
   00032: 
   00033: function safeStr(v) {
   00034:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   00035: }
   00036: 
   00037: function todayYMDLocal() {
   00038:   const d = new Date();
   00039:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00040: }
   00041: 
   00042: function isYMD(s) {
   00043:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00044: }
   00045: 
>> 00046: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   00047:   const m = String(ymd || "")
   00048:     .trim()
   00049:     .match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00050:   if (!m) return "";
   00051:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
   00052: }
   00053: 
   00054: function ymdToUTCDate(ymd) {
   00055:   const m = String(ymd || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00056:   if (!m) return null;
   00057:   return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
   00058: }
   00059: 
   00060: function diffDaysUTC(aYmd, bYmd) {
   00061:   const a = ymdToUTCDate(aYmd);
   00062:   const b = ymdToUTCDate(bYmd);
   00063:   if (!a || !b) return NaN;
   00064:   return Math.floor((b.getTime() - a.getTime()) / 86400000);
   00065: }
   00066: 
   00067: function toGrupo2(n) {
   00068:   const v = Number(n);
   00069:   if (!Number.isFinite(v)) return "";
   00070:   return pad2(v);
   00071: }
   00072: 
   00073: function isValidGrupo(n) {
   00074:   const v = Number(n);
   00075:   return Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 1 && v <= 25;
   00076: }
   00077: 
   00078: function sortPTBR(a, b) {
   00079:   return String(a || "").localeCompare(String(b || ""), "pt-BR", { sensitivity: "base" });
   00080: }
   00081: 
   00082: /**
   00083:  * Normaliza entradas como:
   00084:  * "09h" / "09HS" / "9" / "09:10" -> "09:00" ou "09:10"
   00085:  */
   00086: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00087:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00088:   if (!s0) return "";
   00089: 
   00090:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00091:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00092:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   00093: 
   00094:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00095:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   00096: 
   00097:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00098:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   00099: 
   00100:   return s0.trim();
   00101: }
   00102: 
   00103: /**
   00104:  * Converte "09:00" -> 540
   00105:  */
   00106: function hourToMinutes(hhmm) {
   00107:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
   00108:   const m = String(s).match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00109:   if (!m) return null;
   00110:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
   00111:   const mm = Number(m[2]);
   00112:   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || !Number.isFinite(mm)) return null;
   00113:   return hh * 60 + mm;
   00114: }
   00115: 
   00116: /**
   00117:  * Converte um label de loteria (closeHour) pra bucket "09h"
   00118:  * Aceita "09:00" / "09h" / "09HS" / "9" etc.
   00119:  */
   00120: function toHourBucket(value) {
   00121:   const hhmm = normalizeHourLike(value);
   00122:   const m = String(hhmm).match(/^(\d{2}):/);
   00123:   if (!m) return null;
   00124:   return `${m[1]}h`;
   00125: }

   01060:                     </td>
   01061:                   </tr>
   01062:                 );
   01063:               })}
   01064: 
   01065:               {!loading && (!rows || !rows.length) ? (
   01066:                 <tr>
   01067:                   <td
   01068:                     colSpan={6}
   01069:                     style={{
   01070:                       padding: 14,
   01071:                       textAlign: "center",
   01072:                       color: "rgba(233,233,233,0.7)",
   01073:                       whiteSpace: "normal",
   01074:                     }}
   01075:                   >
   01076:                     Sem dados para exibir.
   01077:                   </td>
   01078:                 </tr>
   01079:               ) : null}
   01080:             </tbody>
   01081:           </table>
   01082:         </div>
   01083:       </div>
   01084:     </div>
   01085:   );
   01086: }

   00233: function isFederalInput(scope) {
   00234:   const up = safeStr(scope).toUpperCase();
   00235:   if (!up) return false;
   00236:   if (up === SCOPE_FEDERAL) return true;
   00237:   if (FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(up)) return true;
   00238:   const compact = up.replace(/[\s_]+/g, " ").trim();
   00239:   if (FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(compact)) return true;
   00240:   const unders = up.replace(/[\s_]+/g, "_");
   00241:   if (FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(unders)) return true;
   00242:   return false;
   00243: }

   00251:   if (s === SCOPE_RJ || compact === "PTRIO" || compact === "RIO") {
   00252:     return SCOPE_RJ;
   00253:   }
   00254: 
   00255:   if (isFederalInput(s)) {
   00256:     return SCOPE_FEDERAL;
   00257:   }
   00258: 
   00259:   if (s === SCOPE_LOOK || compact === "LOOK" || compact === "GO") {
   00260:     return SCOPE_LOOK;

   00010: /**
   00011:  * Search — Premium (SEM índice)
   00012:  * - 2 dígitos => dezena (últimos 2)  ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00013:  * - 3 dígitos => centena (últimos 3) ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00014:  * - 4 dígitos => milhar (4)         ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00015:  *

   00017:  * - 7º prêmio = CENTENA (3 dígitos) e NÃO pode virar 4 dígitos.

   00067:   return String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");

   00074: function extractLastNDigits(v, n) {
   00075:   const digits = normalizeDigitsOnly(v);
   00076:   if (!digits) return null;
   00077:   const last = digits.slice(-n).padStart(n, "0");
   00078:   const re = new RegExp(`^\\d{${n}}$`);
   00079:   return re.test(last) ? last : null;
   00080: }

   00082: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00083:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00084:   if (!s0) return "";
   00085:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00086: 
   00087:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00088:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   00089: 
   00090:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00091:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   00092: 
   00093:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00094:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   00095: 
   00096:   return s0.trim();
   00097: }
   00098: 
   00099: // Bucket "09h", "11h"…
   00100: function toHourBucket(value) {
   00101:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00102:   if (!s || s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return null;
   00103: 
   00104:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   00105:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}h`;
   00106: 
   00107:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00108:   if (m1) return `${pad2(m1[1])}h`;
   00109: 
   00110:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00111:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}h`;
   00112: 
   00113:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00114:   if (m3) return `${pad2(m3[1])}h`;
   00115: 
   00116:   return null;
   00117: }

   00119: // Dia da semana por UTC
   00120: function dowFromYmdUTC(ymd) {
   00121:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return "";
   00122:   const [y, m, d] = ymd.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
   00123:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   00124:   const dow = dt.getUTCDay();
   00125:   const labels = ["Dom", "Seg", "Ter", "Qua", "Qui", "Sex", "Sáb"];
   00126:   return labels[dow] || "";
   00127: }

   00490: /* =========================
   00491:    Pool (concorrência limitada)

   00493: 
   00494: async function runPool(items, worker, concurrency = 3) {
   00495:   const queue = [...items];
   00496:   const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, concurrency) }, async () => {
   00497:     while (queue.length) {
   00498:       const it = queue.shift();
   00499:       // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
   00500:       await worker(it);
   00501:     }
   00502:   });
   00503:   await Promise.all(runners);
   00504: }

src/pages/Top3/top3.formatters.js

--- LINHAS 1 A 173 ---

   00001: // src/pages/Top3/top3.formatters.js
   00002: 
   00003: /* =========================
   00004:    Helpers puros (datas/horas/texto)
   00005: ========================= */
   00006: 
   00007: export function pad2(n) {
   00008:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00009: }
   00010: 
   00011: export function safeStr(v) {
   00012:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   00013: }
   00014: 
   00015: export function isYMD(s) {
   00016:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
   00017:   const m = str.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00018:   if (!m) return false;
   00019: 
   00020:   const y = Number(m[1]);
   00021:   const mo = Number(m[2]);
   00022:   const d = Number(m[3]);
   00023: 
   00024:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d));
   00025:   return (
   00026:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
   00027:     dt.getUTCMonth() === mo - 1 &&
   00028:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
   00029:   );
   00030: }
   00031: 
>> 00032: export function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   00033:   const m = String(ymd || "")
   00034:     .trim()
   00035:     .match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00036:   if (!m) return safeStr(ymd);
   00037:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
   00038: }
   00039: 
   00040: export function brToYMD(br) {
   00041:   const m = String(br || "")
   00042:     .trim()
   00043:     .match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
   00044:   if (!m) return null;
   00045: 
   00046:   const ymd = `${m[3]}-${m[2]}-${m[1]}`;
   00047:   return isYMD(ymd) ? ymd : null;
   00048: }
   00049: 
>> 00050: function dateToLocalYMD(d) {
   00051:   if (!(d instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;
   00052: 
   00053:   const ymd = `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
   00054:     d.getDate()
   00055:   )}`;
   00056: 
   00057:   return isYMD(ymd) ? ymd : null;
   00058: }
   00059: 
   00060: export function normalizeToYMD(input) {
   00061:   if (input == null) return null;
   00062: 
   00063:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
>> 00064:     return dateToLocalYMD(input.toDate());
   00065:   }
   00066: 
   00067:   if (
   00068:     typeof input === "object" &&
   00069:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) ||
   00070:       Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
   00071:   ) {
   00072:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds))
   00073:       ? Number(input.seconds)
   00074:       : Number(input._seconds);
   00075: 
>> 00076:     return dateToLocalYMD(new Date(sec * 1000));
   00077:   }
   00078: 
   00079:   if (input instanceof Date) {
>> 00080:     return dateToLocalYMD(input);
   00081:   }
   00082: 
   00083:   if (typeof input === "number" && Number.isFinite(input)) {
   00084:     const ms = input > 1e12 ? input : input * 1000;
>> 00085:     return dateToLocalYMD(new Date(ms));
   00086:   }
   00087: 
   00088:   const s = safeStr(input);
   00089:   if (!s) return null;
   00090: 
   00091:   if (/^\d{10,13}$/.test(s)) {
   00092:     const n = Number(s);
   00093:     const ms = s.length >= 13 ? n : n * 1000;
>> 00094:     return dateToLocalYMD(new Date(ms));
   00095:   }
   00096: 
   00097:   const isoFull = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00098:   if (isoFull) {
   00099:     const ymd = `${isoFull[1]}-${isoFull[2]}-${isoFull[3]}`;
   00100:     return isYMD(ymd) ? ymd : null;
   00101:   }
   00102: 
   00103:   const isoPrefix = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})[T\s]/);
   00104:   if (isoPrefix) {
   00105:     const ymd = `${isoPrefix[1]}-${isoPrefix[2]}-${isoPrefix[3]}`;
   00106:     return isYMD(ymd) ? ymd : null;
   00107:   }
   00108: 
   00109:   const isoSlash = s.match(/^(\d{4})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{2})(?:\D.*)?$/);
   00110:   if (isoSlash) {
   00111:     const ymd = `${isoSlash[1]}-${isoSlash[2]}-${isoSlash[3]}`;
   00112:     return isYMD(ymd) ? ymd : null;
   00113:   }
   00114: 
   00115:   const compact = s.match(/^(\d{4})(\d{2})(\d{2})$/);
   00116:   if (compact) {
   00117:     const ymd = `${compact[1]}-${compact[2]}-${compact[3]}`;
   00118:     return isYMD(ymd) ? ymd : null;
   00119:   }
   00120: 
   00121:   const y = brToYMD(s);
   00122:   if (y) return y;
   00123: 
   00124:   return null;
   00125: }
   00126: 
   00127: export function todayYMDLocal() {
   00128:   const d = new Date();
   00129:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00130: }
   00131: 
   00132: export function addDaysYMD(ymd, deltaDays) {
   00133:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   00134:   if (!isYMD(y)) return y;
   00135: 
   00136:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
   00137:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
   00138:   const dt = new Date(base);
   00139: 
   00140:   dt.setUTCDate(dt.getUTCDate() + Number(deltaDays || 0));
   00141: 
   00142:   return `${dt.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(dt.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
   00143:     dt.getUTCDate()
   00144:   )}`;
   00145: }
   00146: 
   00147: function isValidHourMinute(hh, mm) {
   00148:   const h = Number(hh);
   00149:   const m = Number(mm);
   00150: 
   00151:   return (
   00152:     Number.isFinite(h) &&
   00153:     Number.isFinite(m) &&
   00154:     h >= 0 &&
   00155:     h <= 23 &&
   00156:     m >= 0 &&
   00157:     m <= 59
   00158:   );
   00159: }
   00160: 
   00161: /**
   00162:  * Normaliza diversos formatos para "HH:MM".
   00163:  */
   00164: export function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00165:   const s0 = safeStr(value);
   00166:   if (!s0) return "";
   00167: 
   00168:   const s = s0
   00169:     .replace(/\s+/g, "")
   00170:     .replace(/[–—]/g, "-")
   00171:     .toLowerCase();
   00172: 
   00173:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);

   00022: function toPercent(score) {
   00023:   const n = Number(score);
   00024:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
   00025:   const pct = n <= 1 ? n * 100 : n;
   00026:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct));
   00027: }
   00028:

   00031:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g <= 0) return "—";
   00032:   return String(Math.trunc(g)).padStart(2, "0");
   00033: }

   00035: function normalizeMilharStr(v) {
   00036:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
   00037:   if (!s) return "";
   00038:   const dig = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
   00039:   if (!dig) return "";
   00040:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
   00041: }

   00060:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-");

   00062: }

   00064: function addDaysYMDLocal(ymd, amount) {
   00065:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();

   00067: 
   00068:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-").map(Number);

   00070: 
   00071:   date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + Number(amount || 0));

   00074:     date.getUTCFullYear(),
   00075:     String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
   00076:     String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
   00077:   ].join("-");
   00078: }

   00099: function hourBucketToSortValue(hour) {
   00100:   const s = String(hour || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   00101:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?h?$/);
   00102:   if (!m) return -1;
   00103:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
   00104:   const mm = Number(m[2] || 0);
   00105:   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || !Number.isFinite(mm)) return -1;
   00106:   return hh * 60 + mm;
   00107: }

   00109: function wrapToDezena2(n) {
   00110:   const x = Number(n);
   00111:   if (!Number.isFinite(x)) return "";
   00112:   const d = ((x % 100) + 100) % 100;
   00113:   return String(d).padStart(2, "0");

   00116: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo) {
   00117:   const g = Number(grupo);
   00118:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
   00119: 
   00120:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
   00121:   const out = [];
   00122:

   00124:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
   00125:   }
   00126: 
   00127:   return out;
   00128: }

   00152: }

   00022: function toPercent(score) {
   00023:   const n = Number(score);
   00024:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
   00025:   const pct = n <= 1 ? n * 100 : n;
   00026:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct));
   00027: }
   00028: 
   00029: function formatGrupo(grupo) {
   00030:   const g = Number(grupo);
   00031:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g <= 0) return "—";
   00032:   return String(Math.trunc(g)).padStart(2, "0");
   00033: }
   00034: 
   00035: function normalizeMilharStr(v) {
   00036:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
   00037:   if (!s) return "";
   00038:   const dig = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
   00039:   if (!dig) return "";
   00040:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
   00041: }
   00042: 
   00043: function centenaFromMilhar(m4) {
   00044:   const s = normalizeMilharStr(m4);
   00045:   return s ? s.slice(-3) : "";
   00046: }
   00047: 
   00048: function dezenaFromMilhar(m4) {
   00049:   const s = normalizeMilharStr(m4);
   00050:   return s ? s.slice(-2) : "";
   00051: }
   00052: 
   00053: function isYMD(s) {
   00054:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00055: }
   00056: 
>> 00057: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   00058:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
   00059:   if (!isYMD(s)) return s || "—";
   00060:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-");
   00061:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
   00062: }
   00063: 
   00064: function formatYmdHour(ymd, hour) {
   00065:   const y = String(ymd || "").trim();
   00066:   const h = String(hour || "").trim();
>> 00067:   if (y && h) return `${ymdToBR(y)} ${h}`;
>> 00068:   if (y) return ymdToBR(y);
   00069:   if (h) return h;
   00070:   return "—";
   00071: }

   00073: function hourBucketToSortValue(hour) {
   00074:   const s = String(hour || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   00075:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?h?$/);
   00076:   if (!m) return -1;
   00077:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
   00078:   const mm = Number(m[2] || 0);
   00079:   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || !Number.isFinite(mm)) return -1;
   00080:   return hh * 60 + mm;
   00081: }
   00082: 
   00083: function wrapToDezena2(n) {
   00084:   const x = Number(n);
   00085:   if (!Number.isFinite(x)) return "";
   00086:   const d = ((x % 100) + 100) % 100;
   00087:   return String(d).padStart(2, "0");
   00088: }

   00090: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo) {
   00091:   const g = Number(grupo);
   00092:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
   00093: 
   00094:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
   00095:   const out = [];
   00096: 
   00097:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
   00098:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
   00099:   }
   00100: 
   00101:   return out;
   00102: }

   00104: function clampColsFromItemMilharesCols(milharesCols, expectedCols = 4, perCol = 5) {
   00105:   const colsArr = Array.isArray(milharesCols) ? milharesCols : [];
   00106:   const out = [];
   00107: 
   00108:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(expectedCols, colsArr.length); i += 1) {
   00109:     const dz = String(colsArr[i]?.dezena || "").trim();
   00110:     const items0 = Array.isArray(colsArr[i]?.items) ? colsArr[i].items : [];
   00111: 
   00112:     const items = items0
   00113:       .map(normalizeMilharStr)
   00114:       .map((m) => (m && /^\d{4}$/.test(m) ? m : ""))
   00115:       .slice(0, perCol);
   00116: 
   00117:     while (items.length < perCol) items.push("");
   00118:     out.push({ dezena: dz, items });
   00119:   }
   00120: 
   00121:   while (out.length < expectedCols) {
   00122:     out.push({ dezena: "", items: Array(perCol).fill("") });
   00123:   }
   00124: 
   00125:   return out.slice(0, expectedCols);
   00126: }

   00001: export async function fallbackBaseSearch({
   00002:   getKingResultsByRange,
   00003:   findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw,
   00004:   addDaysYMD,
>> 00005:   minDate,
   00006:   lotteryKey,
   00007:   targetYmd,
   00008:   targetHourBucket,
   00009:   uf,
   00010: }) {
   00011:   const key = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   00012:   const safeUf = String(uf || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   00013:   const safeTargetYmd = String(targetYmd || "").trim();
   00014:   const safeTargetHour = String(targetHourBucket || "").trim();
   00015: 
   00016:   if (!safeTargetYmd || !safeTargetHour || !safeUf) {
   00017:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
   00018:   }
   00019: 
   00020:   const searchFrom =
>> 00021:     String(minDate || "").trim() ||
   00022:     (key === "FEDERAL"
   00023:       ? addDaysYMD(safeTargetYmd, -240)
   00024:       : addDaysYMD(safeTargetYmd, -90));
   00025: 
   00026:   const hist =
   00027:     (await getKingResultsByRange({
   00028:       uf: safeUf,
>> 00029:       dateFrom: searchFrom,
>> 00030:       dateTo: safeTargetYmd,
   00031:       mode: "detailed",
   00032:       readPolicy: "server",
   00033:     })) || [];
   00034: 
   00035:   return findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw({
   00036:     draws: hist,
   00037:     lotteryKey: key,
   00038:     targetYmd: safeTargetYmd,
   00039:     targetHourBucket: safeTargetHour,
   00040:   });
   00041: }
   00042: 
   00043: export async function loadHistoryRange({
   00044:   getKingResultsByRange,
   00045:   uf,
>> 00046:   dateFrom,

   00049: }) {

>> 00051:   const safeDateFrom = String(dateFrom || "").trim();
>> 00052:   const safeDateTo = String(dateTo || "").trim();
   00053: 
   00054:   if (!safeUf || !safeDateFrom || !safeDateTo) return [];

   00057:     (await getKingResultsByRange({
   00058:       uf: safeUf,
>> 00059:       dateFrom: safeDateFrom,
>> 00060:       dateTo: safeDateTo,
   00061:       mode: "detailed",
   00062:       readPolicy,
   00063:     })) || []
   00064:   );
   00065: }

   00050:       reasons.push("Frequência muito elevada.");
   00051:       break;
   00052: 
   00053:     case "high":
   00054:       reasons.push("Frequência elevada.");
   00055:       break;
   00056: 
   00057:     case "medium":
   00058:       reasons.push("Frequência intermediária.");
   00059:       break;
   00060: 
   00061:     case "low":
   00062:       reasons.push("Frequência baixa.");
   00063:       break;
   00064: 
   00065:     default:
   00066:       reasons.push("Pouca evidência de frequência.");
   00067:   }
   00068: 
   00069:   return reasons;
   00070: }
   00071: 
   00072: function buildFrequencyEvidence(item = {}, context = {}) {
   00073:   const frequency = normalize(
   00074:     item.frequency ??
   00075:     item.total ??
   00076:     item.count ??
   00077:     item.appearances
   00078:   );
   00079: 
   00080:   const totalUniverse = normalize(
>> 00081:     context.totalDraws ??
   00082:     context.windowSize ??
   00083:     context.sampleSize
   00084:   );
   00085: 
   00086:   const percent =
   00087:     totalUniverse > 0
   00088:       ? normalizePercent((frequency / totalUniverse) * 100)
   00089:       : normalizePercent(
   00090:           item.frequencyPercent ??
   00091:           item.frequencyRate
   00092:         );
   00093: 
   00094:   const level = classifyFrequency(percent);
   00095: 
   00096:   return {
   00097:     module: "frequency",
   00098: 
   00099:     value: percent,
   00100: 
   00101:     evidence: {
   00102:       frequency,
   00103:       percent,
   00104:       level,
   00105:       totalUniverse,
   00106: 
   00107:       window: context.window || null,
   00108:       lotteryKey: context.lotteryKey || null,
   00109:       position: context.position || null,
   00110:       weekday: context.weekday || null,
   00111:       hour: context.hour || null,
   00112:     },
   00113: 
   00114:     reasons: buildReasons(frequency, percent, level),
   00115:   };
   00116: }
   00117: 
   00118: export {
   00119: buildFrequencyEvidence,
   00120: };

   00257:     const actualFirst = actualTop5.find((x) => x.pos === 1)?.grupo ?? null;
   00258: 
   00259:     if (!actualGroups.length || !Number.isFinite(Number(actualFirst))) {
   00260:       continue;
   00261:     }
   00262: 
   00263:     const hasTop5 = pred.some((g) => actualGroups.includes(g));
   00264:     const hasFirst = pred.includes(Number(actualFirst));
   00265: 
   00266:     totalCases += 1;
   00267:     if (hasTop5) hitTop5 += 1;
   00268:     if (hasFirst) hitFirst += 1;
   00269: 
   00270:     const targetHour =
   00271:       found?.hour || found?.slot?.hour || pickDrawHour(nextDraw) || "";
   00272: 
   00273:     const row = ensureHourRow(byTargetHour, targetHour);
   00274:     row.total += 1;
   00275:     if (hasTop5) row.hitTop5 += 1;
   00276:     if (hasFirst) row.hitFirst += 1;
   00277:   }
   00278: 
   00279:   console.log("\n================ BACKTEST TOP3 PT_RIO ================");
>> 00280:   console.log(`Janela: ${from} até ${maxDate}`);

   00282:   console.log(

   00285:       totalCases
   00286:     )}%`
   00287:   );
   00288:   console.log(

   00290:       hitFirst,
   00291:       totalCases
   00292:     )}%`
   00293:   );
   00294:   console.log("------------------------------------------------------");
   00295: 
   00296:   for (const [hour, row] of Array.from(byTargetHour.entries()).sort()) {

>> 00572:             dateFrom: ymdFrom,
>> 00573:             dateTo: ymdTo,
   00574:             closeHour: null,
   00575:             positions: positionsArrStable,
   00576:             mode: serviceMode,
   00577:             readPolicy: "server",
   00578:           });
   00579:         } else if (mode === "day") {
   00580:           serviceMode = "detailed";
   00581: 
   00582:           draws = await getKingResultsByDate({
   00583:             uf,
   00584:             date: effectiveDayYmd,
   00585:             closeHour: null,
   00586:             positions: positionsArrStable,
   00587:             readPolicy: "server",
   00588:           });
   00589:         } else {
   00590:           draws = [];
   00591:         }
   00592: 
   00593:         if (!mounted || mySeq !== refreshSeqRef.current) return;
   00594: 
   00595:         if (rangeBlocked) setRangeBlocked(false);
   00596: 
   00597:         let unique = dedupeDrawsLogicalPreferBest(draws).map(normalizeDrawForCharts);
   00598: 
   00599:         if (bucketNorm) {
   00600:           unique = unique.filter((d) => toHourBucketLabel(d?.close_hour) === bucketNorm);
   00601:         }
   00602: 
   00603:         setDrawsRaw(unique);
   00604: 
   00605:         const hasAnyPrize = unique.some((d) => Array.isArray(d?.prizes) && d.prizes.length);
   00606: 
   00607:         if (serviceMode === "detailed") {
   00608:           if (hasAnyPrize) {
   00609:             const built = buildRanking(unique);
   00610:             setData(built.ranking || []);
   00611: 
   00612:             const palpiteBuilt = buildPalpite(unique, {
   00613:               minDraws: 400,
   00614:               maxDraws: 2000,
   00615:             });
   00616: 
   00617:             setMeta((prev) => ({
   00618:               ...prev,
   00619:               top3: built.top3 || [],
   00620:               totalOcorrencias: Number(built.totalOcorrencias || 0),
>> 00621:               totalDraws: unique.length,
   00622:               mode,
   00623:               date: mode === "day" ? effectiveDayYmd : null,
>> 00624:               dateFrom: mode === "range" ? ymdFrom : null,
>> 00625:               dateTo: mode === "range" ? ymdTo : null,
   00626: 
   00627:               palpitesByGrupo: palpiteBuilt?.palpitesByGrupo || {},
   00628:               palpiteSampleDrawsUsed: Number(palpiteBuilt?.sampleDrawsUsed || 0),
   00629:               palpiteUsedBucket: palpiteBuilt?.usedBucket ?? null,
   00630: 
>> 00631:               bounds: boundsSnapshot,
   00632:               suggestedRange: {
>> 00633:                 from: boundsSnapshot?.minYmd || null,
>> 00634:                 to: boundsSnapshot?.maxYmd || null,
   00635:               },
   00636: 
   00637:               serviceMode,
   00638:               hydrating: false,
   00639:             }));
   00640:           } else {
   00641:             setData([]);
   00642:             setMeta((prev) => ({
   00643:               ...prev,

>> 00646:               totalDraws: unique.length,

   00665:           }
   00666:

   00668:         }
   00669: 
   00670:         setData([]);
   00671:         setMeta((prev) => ({
   00672:           ...prev,
   00673:           top3: [],
   00674:           totalOcorrencias: 0,
>> 00675:           totalDraws: unique.length,
   00676:           mode,
   00677:           date: null,

>> 00679:           dateTo: mode === "range" ? ymdTo : null,
   00680:

   00682:           palpiteSampleDrawsUsed: 0,
   00683:           palpiteUsedBucket: null,
   00684: 
>> 00685:           bounds: boundsSnapshot,

   00689:           },
   00690: 
   00691:           serviceMode: "aggregated",
   00692:           hydrating: false,
   00693:         }));
   00694:       } catch (e) {
   00695:         if (!mounted) return;
   00696:         if (refreshSeqRef.current && mySeq !== refreshSeqRef.current) return;
   00697: 
   00698:         if (
   00699:           mode === "range" &&
   00700:           isIndexErrorMessage(e) &&
   00701:           Number.isFinite(rangeDays) &&
   00702:           rangeDays > RANGE_FALLBACK_LIMIT_DAYS
   00703:         ) {
   00704:           setRangeBlocked(true);
   00705:         }
   00706: 
   00707:         if (hard) {
   00708:           setDrawsRaw([]);
   00709:           setData([]);
   00710:         }
   00711: 
   00712:         setMeta((prev) => ({
   00713:           ...prev,
   00714:           hydrating: false,
   00715:         }));
   00716: 
   00717:         setError(e instanceof Error ? e : new Error("Erro desconhecido"));
   00718:       } finally {
   00719:         if (mounted && mySeq === refreshSeqRef.current) {
   00720:           setLoading(false);
   00721:         }
   00722:       }
   00723:     }
   00724: 
   00725:     if (mode === "none") {
   00726:       setError(null);
   00727:       setDrawsRaw([]);
   00728:       setData([]);
   00729:       setMeta(INITIAL_META);
   00730:       setLoading(false);
   00731:       return () => {
   00732:         mounted = false;
   00733:       };
   00734:     }
   00735: 
   00736:     const isHard = lastHardKeyRef.current !== hardKey;
   00737:     lastHardKeyRef.current = hardKey;
   00738: 
   00739:     load({ hard: isHard });
   00740: 
   00741:     return () => {
   00742:       mounted = false;
   00743:     };
   00744:   }, [
   00745:     mode,
   00746:     uf,
